Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The modules variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 619 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 958 and intersection form of rank 3.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 619 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the modules structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 958. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
